# SYSTEM MAP — btc-5m-twap

**Revision 2026-10-29-a.** Written by the Architect; the Executor never edits it. This is a
**state** document: what exists right now. It holds no mission, no rules and no history.

- The CANON (Architect's project instructions, not in this repository) holds the mission, the
  fair-value model, the measured facts and the phase gates.
- `BTC-EXECUTOR-INSTRUCTIONS.md` holds how the Executor works.
- **This map never restates a CANON §1.2 formula.**

---

Hashes are of the file as committed on `main` at revision date, verified by the Architect against
`origin/main` — not copied from a report. Changing a `frozen` row requires a TZ that authorizes
it and a new map revision. **The six recorder files are frozen because the live capture runs
them**: an unauthorized edit is a change to a running instrument. **`pfair.py` is frozen because
every score this project will ever quote comes out of it** — both links and the four measured
tables. `tz07a-variance-time.py`, `tz07b-settlement-dispersion.py`, `tz08a-out-of-sample.py`,
`tz09-disk-inventory.py`, `tz10b-sigma-or-link.py`, `tz11a-student-link.py`, `tz12-sized-gate.py`
and `tz13-sized-gate-test3.py` are frozen because a committed measurement came out of each — for
`tz12-sized-gate.py` that is `SIGMA_LOG_NU`, the member-bootstrap spread of `log ν̂`, and the sized
gate; for `tz13-sized-gate-test3.py`, the first verdict under it; for `tz14-quote-inventory.py`, the
first read of the book — and it also holds **the only implementation of CANON §1.1's taker fee,
`fee_pp`, and of the executable touch, `touch_of`**, so every later TZ imports them from it and none
writes them again. `tz15-phase2-gate.py` is frozen for Phase 2's first reading, and because it holds
**the only implementation of the exact Poisson-binomial tail, `pb_upper`, its critical count, `crit`,
and the after-fee selection, `select`**, which a later TZ imports and never writes again.
`tz16a-phase2-decisive-gate.py` is frozen for Phase 2's first decisive look, and because it holds
**the only implementation of the look's arithmetic — `up`, `low`, `critical_upper`, `critical_lower`,
`look_constants`, `read_look`, `dependence` and `residual_dependence`** — which TZ-16a §4.2 requires the
second look to import and never write again.
`tz17-settlement-chain.py` is frozen for the backup track's first answer, B0, and because it holds
**the first committed request outside the recorder that the venue's edge serves** — `Accept:
application/json` and a `User-Agent` of its own, where Python's default agent is refused (§6).
**`tz18a-chainbook-capture.py` is frozen because the chain book service runs its copy byte for byte**
— an unauthorized edit is a change to a running instrument, as for the recorder — and for B1's
answer; its `build_request` is the second such request, `User-Agent: btc-5m-twap-tz18a`.
`tz06-calibration.py` is frozen at
its post-PR-#9 hash: it carries `after` and `need`, and at their defaults the run is the one TZ-06
ran. **The eight unit files are frozen because systemd holds them**: `/etc/systemd/system/` holds each byte for
byte, as every host gate asserts with `cmp`, so an unauthorized edit is a change to a running unit's definition —
or, for the chain book's, disabled since TZ-26, to its fallback's; each archive's service is started by its timer
alone.
`tz20-capture-under-systemd.py` is frozen for the deployment proof that came out of it, and
`tz21-recorder-memory.py` for TZ-21's: the bounded recorder serves every slice the old code served, byte for byte.
`tz23-maker-m0.py` is frozen for M0's first look, and because it holds **the only implementation of the makers' side
of a taker fill, `maker_terms`, the 20% rebate, `rebate`, a member's sums by band, `aggregate`, the anytime-valid
thresholds, `gate_constants`, and the reading, `reading`**, with the set rule, `qualify` and `candidates` — which M0's
second look imports and never writes again (§5).
`tz24-nesting-b2.py` is frozen for B2's reading, VIOLATION, and because it holds **the only implementation of the
nested pair's arithmetic — the dominated pair of a window, `pairs_of`, its settlement payoff, `payoff`, a leg's cost
after the taker fee under both ways the venue could collect it, `costs`, the firm and loose classes, `classify`, and
the reading, `reading_of`, with NONE's alternative `theta1` and its `power`** — which B3's measurement imports and
never writes again (§5).
**`tz26-nesting-b3-capture.py` is frozen because the pair book's unit runs its copy byte for byte** — an
unauthorized edit is a change to a running instrument, as for the recorder — and for B3's capture; its
`build_request` is the third request the venue's edge serves, `User-Agent: btc-5m-twap-tz26`, and its store's
layout — `next_stream_path`'s names, `stream_bytes_total`'s count, `stream_off_reason`'s floor and cap — is what
TZ-27 reads that store by (§2.5).
**`tz27-pairbook-archive.py` is frozen for the archive it carried out, and because TZ-28's and TZ-34's instruments load it
byte for byte, asserted by its SHA-256, and both archives' timers run it:** its set rule, `inventory`, its proofs,
`upload_set`, `verify_release` and `delete_set`, and its readings of the restart, `restart_reading`, `counter_reading` and
`resume_decision`, are the pair book's archive's, its proofs and its audit hook the reward book's archive's too, and are
written nowhere else — an unauthorized edit is a change to a running instrument.
**`tz28-pairbook-archive-timer.py` is frozen because both archives' timers run its copy byte for byte** — the pair book's
as its `run`, the reward book's through TZ-34's, which loads it by its SHA-256 — an unauthorized edit is a change to a
running instrument — and for the deployment it carried: its `run` loads `tz27-pairbook-archive.py` from beside itself by
its SHA-256, and its idle stretch, `idle_wait`, its ledger and its resume rule are both archives' and are written nowhere
else.
`tz29-maker-m0-second-look.py` is frozen for M0's reading over both looks, NO EARN, which closed the maker track for
its window (§4, §5): the first look read from the forensic store, the sum over both looks and the fetch held to the
idle stretch are written in it and nowhere else.
`tz30-nesting-b3-measure.py` is frozen for B3's reading, NO CAPTURE, which closed the backup track (§4, §5): the replay of
a window's streams at venue time, the capture rule, every book rebuilt against the stored replies and the reading over
eight blocks are written in it and nowhere else.
`tz31-who-earns.py` is frozen for W0's reading, W-EARN, which opened W1 (§4, §5): the makers' rows of a market — its
full feed less its takers' rows, as multisets — each wallet's own position from its rows with their side flipped, the two
named groups' sums over each market's life and the fetch held to the idle stretch are written in it and nowhere else.
`tz32-our-quoting.py` is frozen for W1's reading, W1-NO-EARN, which closed the W track (§4, §5): the replay of a
five-minute leg's streams at venue time — its Up book rebuilt from the stored frames, a match read from the Up token's
own book frame of the same stamp, our quotes queued at their level, filled by a match's sweep or, once the 150 ms
taker delay has passed, by an order that crosses them, refused where they would cross, cancelled through a one-way
latency — under nine variants is written in it and nowhere else.
**`tz33-reward-book-capture.py` is frozen because the reward book's unit runs its copy byte for byte** — an unauthorized
edit is a change to a running instrument — and for L0's capture: its selection rule, `run_rule` through `LiveFetch` or
`StoredFetch`, its slot schedule and its store's layout — the names under `/var/lib/btc-rewardbook/<D0>/`, the stream
files' sum taken again at every slot's decision, the floor and the cap that keep a slot's stream off — are what L0's
measurement and the reward book's archive read that store by (§2.6), and its `build_request` is the fourth request the
venue's edge serves, `User-Agent: btc-5m-twap-tz33`.
**`tz34-rewardbook-archive-timer.py` is frozen because the reward book's archive's timer runs its copy byte for byte**
— an unauthorized edit is a change to a running instrument — and for the deployment it carried: its `run` loads
`tz28-pairbook-archive-timer.py`, and through it `tz27-pairbook-archive.py`, from beside itself by their SHA-256; its set
of a store's closed slots, `inventory`, its removal while the reward book is quiet, `quiet`, `removal_wait` and
`remove_set`, and its readings — G-TRIGGER, G-SET, G-NEXT and G-STILL as pure functions — are written in it and nowhere
else.
**Every one of the forty-eight file rows above was read byte for byte by the Architect on 2026-10-29**, by fetching
the public repository and hashing each path — the forty-five at `origin/main` `cfa0bf5d8d25bcbd4701b384ec7e61cdae314fc8`,
the TZ-34 report's commit, and `research/tz34-rewardbook-archive-timer.py`, `deploy/systemd/btc-rewardbook-archive.service`
and `deploy/systemd/btc-rewardbook-archive.timer` at PR #34's head `6a615257277d3c94dafd4d39783499560a344648`, the three
files its merge adds — **48 of 48 equal to the values printed here, lines and bytes included**, the 46 `frozen` rows and

PR #34 adds are byte-identical to TZ-34's Appendix A, `1d8aa514…`, `a9abb1f0…` and `bf5af025…`.

entered git history. A bare clone of it is one pack of `2,785` KiB, `719` objects, read by
`git count-objects` on 2026-10-24 at PR #30's merge; it holds text alone and stays that way.

## 2. Data

### 2.1 The observation set — the only dataset that exists

| field | value |
|---|---|
| location | Release `TZ-01a validated observation set`, tag `tz-01a-dataset`, public |
| asset | `twap-divergence-observations.parquet` |
| size | 76,818,669 bytes |
| SHA-256 | `229a944f2d5111c3e68b1fa0630f8e8658356147f9669d18665e575b60f3716b` |
| rows | 1,051,170 — 210,234 intervals × 5 checkpoints |
| checkpoints | `tau` ∈ {240, 180, 120, 90, 60} seconds remaining |
| window | 2024-09 … 2026-08, 730 days |
| source | `data.binance.vision`, spot `BTCUSDT`, 1-second klines, 63,072,000 bars, each monthly archive checksum-verified |
| gaps | 0 missing bars, 0 forward-fills; 210,240 intervals found, 210,234 used |

Anyone can fetch and verify it with no token:
`curl -sL https://github.com/seahomebatumi-ai/btc-5m-twap/releases/download/tz-01a-dataset/twap-divergence-observations.parquet | sha256sum`

**Columns (25).** Keys `interval_open_ts`, `month`, `tau`, `t`. Inputs `K`, `S_t`, `naive_move`,
`twap_so_far`, `state`. Volatility `sigma_pre`, `sigma_live`, `sd_remaining_pre`,
`sd_remaining_live`. Prices `p_twap_pre`, `p_naive_pre`, `p_twap_live`, `p_naive_live`.
Settlement proxies `twap_1s`, `twap_30s`, `twap_60s`, `outcome_1s`, `outcome_30s`, `outcome_60s`.
Integrity `n_missing_bars`, `prior_filled_bars`.

**The price data and the integrity accounting remain valid.** The `p_twap_*` columns implement
the superseded `p_interval` model and the `outcome_*` columns are invalid labels — see §2.2. This
set is Binance data and can no longer supply a label to anything.

### 2.2 The outcome labels are invalid — withdrawn 2026-09-10

`outcome_1s`, `outcome_30s` and `outcome_60s` are all **full-interval averages** of Binance
prices compared against the interval open, at 1 / 30 / 60-second sampling.

**No Polymarket settlement rule has ever had that shape.** Before 2026-08-07 the venue compared a
single close against a single open. Since 2026-08-07 it compares two readings of a Chainlink
trailing-TWAP feed — one at the close, one at the open — with a 30-second lookback until
2026-08-14 and 60 seconds after it.

The three labels are therefore withdrawn, along with every score computed against them. They stay
in the Parquet file for forensics and are never used again. The 4.147% disagreement between
`outcome_1s` and `outcome_60s` is a fact about two invalid labels and carries no information.

Correct labels are the venue's own resolutions, captured per interval as `resolution.json`. TZ-04b
read 200 of them and TZ-06 read 400.

### 2.3 Live capture — running since 2026-10-04 07:39 UTC as `btc-recorder.service`, on TZ-21's bounded code since 22:22:07 UTC; Tier C scored on 1,200 intervals by TZ-15 and on 2,400 by TZ-16a

**Tier A** has captured from **2026-09-10 10:21:09 UTC**, with the gap below, into `/var/lib/btc-recorder/**`
on the VPS — outside the repository, never in git history. Streams, one gzipped file per interval:
`twap60`, `twap30`, `chainlink`, `binance`, plus `gamma.json` (S6, the market document at
`T0 + 5`), `resolution.json` (S7, the venue's settled document), `runtime.jsonl` and
`manifest.json`. Reports carry the venue payload's own `timestamp` in milliseconds and
`full_accuracy_value` at full precision; the manifest is a pure function of the interval directory
and of the schema of the day it was written.

**Tier C** — seven order-book snapshots per interval per token id, fourteen reads, at
`tau ∈ {240, 180, 120, 90, 60, 30, 10}` — was added **2026-09-12 09:53:05 UTC** on commit
`4216c04`, pid `228592`. It stores each reply verbatim with its HTTP status in
`quotes.jsonl.gz`, and the manifest gained `quotes_complete` and `quote_offsets_ms`, both
independent of `complete`. The Tier B order-book probe ran once, for 60 minutes, and retained
exactly one interval. **The manifest exception:** a manifest carries the capture's own accounting — message counts,
gaps, disconnects, clock offsets, `complete`, `quotes_complete`, and each Tier C read's `tau`, token, status and offset —
and no price, size, fill or outcome (`research/recorder/manifest.py`, the return at lines 217 to 233), so a TZ barred
from every other file of the capture may still read manifests.

**The capture stopped on 2026-10-02** (TZ-19 report §3). The recorder's last record is a `clock` record at
`recv_ns` `1790940944342950552`, 11:35:44 UTC, after 20.07 days from its `start` record at
`1789206785264516934`; its newest interval directory is `1790940900`, and none exists for any `T0` from
`1790941200`, 11:40 UTC, on. It ran inside root's login session 7379 and ended with it (§7 item 85).
**Every interval from `1790941200` to the next start is lost**: RTDS keeps no history (CANON §1.1).
**TZ-20 restarted it on 2026-10-04 at 07:39:16 UTC** as `btc-recorder.service`, from the detached worktree
`/root/btc-recorder-svc` at `4216c04` (§6). Its `startup` record holds the gap — from `1790940978479700775`,
2026-10-02 11:36:18.479 UTC, the old process's last frame on disk, to `1791099559177430242`, 2026-10-04
07:39:19.177, `158,580.698` s, recorded and not filled: **the `527` intervals `1790941200` … `1791099000` were
never captured.** TZ-20's B-KILL-REC restarted it once more at 08:27:02, a recorded gap of `6.934` s; at its
`I final` it ran as pid `4067771`, `NRestarts` `1`, and `runtime.jsonl` held `60,786` records. **TZ-21 moved the
unit's tree to its branch head `e3975b5` and restarted it once**, on 2026-10-04: B-MOVE at 22:19:04 UTC, then one
`SIGTERM` at 22:22:02.109 by `systemctl kill`; the old process's last frame at 22:22:01.502, the new `start` record
at 22:22:07.735 on `e3975b5`, a recorded gap of `6.771` s. Interval `1791152400` lost `complete`, as TZ-21 intended,
and the five after it were `complete` and `quotes_complete`, their slices equal to the old code's. At TZ-21's
`I final`, 23:08:22 UTC, the recorder ran as pid `4148534`, `NRestarts` `2`, and `runtime.jsonl` held `62,466`
records (§6, memory).

**Tier C has been scored twice:** by TZ-15, on TZ-14's 1,200 intervals, and by TZ-16a, on the first
2,400 qualifying slots after `1789669800`. **The reserve is released, 2026-10-04:** the second look TZ-16a §4.2 pre-registered is withdrawn unrun
(CANON §1.5; §5 here), so no span of this capture is held for a Phase 2 reading. The slots after TZ-16a's
2,400th member, `1790452200`, are the only span of this capture whose quotes no TZ has read; their labels TZ-23
read from the venue's documents, up to `1791241800`, M0's first look's last member, and TZ-29 those of the next 4,032,
to `1792451400` (§5). The span's five-minute books are also
stored by the chain book, from 2026-09-23 17:39:50 to 2026-10-03 08:56:15 UTC, and again from 2026-10-04 07:39:45 UTC
under its unit. **TZ-23, M0's first look, read nothing of this capture** (above). **TZ-24, B2, read the chain book**
— the `window.json` of every window from `1790184600` to `1791241200` that has a directory and, once its constants
were on disk, the members' `books.jsonl.gz`, read only — and nothing under the recorder's root (above). **TZ-30, B3,
read the pair book** — the `window.json` of B3's 936 candidates and the members' `books.jsonl.gz`, read only, beside
their streams from the archive's Releases — **and `twap60` of each walked candidate's two five-minute markets** under
the recorder's root, for the strikes (above).

**Measured footprint.**

| quantity | exact bytes |
|---|---|
| Tier A per interval, TZ-04b | `76,643` |
| Tier A per interval, TZ-05a over four intervals | `58,651` |
| Tier A per day, the figure every projection uses | `22,073,069` |
| Tier C per interval, stored | `6,171.5` mean |
| Tier C per interval, raw | `61,896.5` mean |
| Tier C per interval, raw, **measured by TZ-14 over 1,200 intervals** | `61,971.6` mean — `74,365,905` over 16,800 replies |
| Tier C per interval, stored, measured by TZ-14 | `6,293.6` mean — `7,552,278` gzip over 1,200 files |
| Tier C per day | `1,777,392` |
| Tier A + Tier C per day | `23,850,461` |
| full order book per day, compressed — why it is never captured | `1,913,827,421` |

The two Tier A per-interval figures differ by 23%; the larger is kept in the projection because it
is the conservative one. **Measured against the filesystem by TZ-09 over one hour: `33,632,842`
bytes/day on disk and `26,784,064` apparent.** The projection understates the on-disk cost by
`9,782,381` bytes/day, because every interval directory holds eight or nine small files and the
filesystem rounds each to 4,096-byte blocks. Venue resolution reaches the recorder a median of `318` s after interval
close; the venue's own `closedTime` is a median of `55` s.

**The book, measured by TZ-14 over 1,200 intervals and 16,800 replies.** Complete: 14 reads at every
member, every one HTTP 200 and parseable, no duplicate, no torn line, `quotes_complete` 1,200 of 1,200.
One payload shape of ten keys — `asks`, `asset_id`, `bids`, `hash`, `last_trade_price`, `market`,
`min_order_size`, `neg_risk`, `tick_size`, `timestamp` — and `asset_id` equal to the requested token
at 16,800 of 16,800. **`bids` arrive ascending and `asks` descending, so position 0 is the worst price
on both sides**: a reader takes `max` over bids and `min` over asks, as `tz14.best_of` does. Reads
land `+39.6` to `+391.1` ms after the checkpoint, median about `52` ms, never negative and never timed
out; the reply's own `timestamp` precedes reception by a median of `21` to `27` ms, with a tail to
`1,311` ms; the host clock's worst offset over the set is `33.946` ms. **The book empties toward the
close:** both sides are quoted at `1.0000` of reads at `tau = 240`, `0.9975` at 180, `0.9358` at 120,
`0.8354` at 90, **`0.6050` at 60**, `0.2117` at 30 and `0.0500` at 10 — and no read is empty on both
sides, so at every checkpoint at least one side can be bought. The median spread is one tick at every
`tau`; at the venue's `min_order_size` of `5` the executable touch differs from the quoted one at 53
bids and 51 asks of 11,125 two-sided reads. **The round trip at the touch — spread plus both taker
fees — falls from `4.119` probability points at `tau = 240` to `1.851` at 60**, because the fee falls
as the price leaves 0.5, which is also why the book empties. The two tokens are one book quoted
twice: `mid_up − (1 − mid_down)` is exactly zero from the 0.25 to the 0.90 quantile at every admitted
`tau`, and one executable free lunch after fees appeared in 8,400 paired checkpoints, worth `0.79`
probability points on 90 shares. **Inside the domain the population is about half the set**: 631,
628, 615, 619 and 621 admissible members at `tau` 240 … 60, only 42 to 54 of them weekend, and at
`tau = 60` 311 of the 621 have both sides of the book quoted.

**Feed cadence, measured TZ-06 over 400 intervals:** `chainlink` and `twap60` both publish at
1 Hz — median gap between consecutive reports `1,000` ms on both, over 232,924 and 232,968 gaps,
a median of 584 reports per member per stream. The maximum gap of `34,000` ms is a recorded
disconnect in a neighbouring directory, not a hole in a member's own capture.

**Feed dispersion, measured TZ-07a over the same 400:** pooled over `3,028,021` one-second-grid
increments at fifteen lags, `g(h) = sigma_h^2 / sigma_1^2` rises from 1 at `h = 1` to `1.9561` at
`h = 20`, and is nearly flat above that — `2.2023` at `h = 300`. The rise is complete by twenty
seconds, so it is not an artifact of long extrapolation. Pooled and median-member readings part
company as `h` grows: `2.2023` against `1.3344` at `h = 300`. `10,779` increments were dropped
under the disconnect rule, contributed by 15 members.

**Settlement-quantity dispersion, measured TZ-07b over the same 400:** `1,384,934` admissible
anchors of `1,390,800`, `5,866` dropped by the same imported disconnect rule and by the same 15
members. `Λ(tau)` — the raw uncentred root mean square of `Y / (sigma * sqrt(H(tau)))`, where `Y`
is the settlement average minus the reading in force at the anchor — reads `1.52376` / `1.49602` /
`1.49628` / `1.47915` / `1.44466` / `1.38581` / `1.21118` at `tau` 240 / 180 / 120 / 90 / 60 / 30 /
10. **This is the dispersion of the variable the pricer's `sd` divides, not of a proxy for it**,
and §5 of that TZ froze it into the pricer.

Three diagnostics carry no threshold and are recorded because a question about **shape** now has
its data. The checkpoint-only reading is above the pooled one at every tau — `1.8184` against
`1.5238` at 240 — and the median-member reading below it. `MAD / 0.674490` and `IQR / 1.348980`
agree with each other to the third decimal and sit below `Λ` by 4% at `tau = 240` and 49% at
`tau = 10`. The empirical tails are lighter than the normal's at one `Λ` and heavier at three, at
every tau, and the gap widens monotonically as `tau` falls.

**Shape, measured TZ-10b over the 753 and over the full 800.** `κ = Λ_u / (MAD / 0.674490)` —
the root mean square of the standardized settlement residual divided by its own robust scale. A
normal gives exactly 1. Under the pricer's own causal `sigma`, `κ` reads
`1.1209` / `1.1328` / `1.1875` / `1.2926` / `1.5005` / `1.9753` / `3.2288` at `tau` 240 … 10 over
the intersection, and within 0.02 of that over the full 800 — 2.7 to 3.0 million anchors per
`tau`. The gradient in `tau` is the finding: the link is near-normal four minutes out and is not
a normal at any scale inside the final minute.

**The `sigma` reading is refuted, three ways — TZ-10b M1, M3 and M4 over the same 753.**
Standardizing by `sigma_post`, the successor interval's realised scale — non-causal, and free of
any estimation lag by construction — leaves `κ` **higher** at 7 of 7 `tau`: `2.1908` against
`1.5005` at 60, `2.8957` against `1.9753` at 30. Within deciles of the realised scale error
`log(sigma_post / sigma_hat)` the size-weighted `κ` is at or above pooled at 5 of 7 `tau`, and the
two extreme deciles read `3.449` and `2.655` at `tau = 30`. Four causal regressors fitted on the
intersection's TZ-06 share give `R²` `0.1090` in sample and **`−0.0189`** out. **A scale mixture
collapses when you condition on its mixing variable; this does not.**

**Where the tail lives — TZ-10b M3, deciles of `sigma_hat`, causal.** Within-stratum `κ` is at or
above the pooled value at 7 of 7 `tau`, and it is strongly ordered: at `tau = 30` the lowest
decile of `sigma_hat` reads `3.227` and the highest `1.119`; at 60, `2.611` against `1.077`. **The
heavy tail sits where the causal volatility estimate is smallest**, which is a decision-time
observable and is why TZ-11a carries an admissibility rule rather than only a link.

**`κ` is not stationary between the two windows, and TZ-10b did not measure it per set.** Re-derived
by the Architect from committed diagnostics: TZ-07b's in-sample table puts `κ` on the TZ-06 400 at
`1.042` at `tau = 240` and `1.961` at 10; TZ-08a V9 puts it on the TZ-08a 400 at `1.22` and `5.26`.
Same statistic, two adjacent day-and-a-half windows, a factor of 2.7 apart at `tau = 10`. TZ-11a M3
has since measured it per set, below, and the calendar below says what the two windows were.

**`κ` maps to a degrees-of-freedom, exactly.** For a Student's `t` with `ν` degrees of freedom the
population `κ` is `1.02257` / `1.03497` / `1.07758` / `1.10256` / `1.12223` / `1.15124` / `1.19826`
/ `1.28780` / `1.52734` / `1.92125` / `2.78935` at `ν` 30 / 20 / 10 / 8 / 7 / 6 / 5 / 4 / 3 / 2.5 /
2.2, computed by the Architect at 40 decimal digits and carrying no threshold. Inverted against the
measurements above, `ν` runs `7.06` / `6.59` / `5.19` / `3.96` / `3.06` / `2.46` / `2.14` at
`tau` 240 … 10 over the intersection — and per set, `17.1` against `4.69` at `tau = 240` and `2.47`
against `2.05` at `tau = 10`. **Below `ν = 3` the kurtosis does not exist and below 2 the variance
does not**: that is the quantitative form of §7 item 22, and it is why a root-mean-square scale
constant cannot be the estimator at short `tau`.

**The Student link, fitted by TZ-11a M1 on the fit set alone.** Over the admissible anchors of the
TZ-06 400 — `100,605` at `tau = 240` to `165,369` at 10 — the maximum-likelihood `ν̂` of a symmetric
Student's `t` reads `9.62070` / `13.7083` / `20.2258` / `16.0482` / `9.51490` / `4.79579` / `2.13489`
and `ŝ` `1.30678` / `1.36658` / `1.40748` / `1.37085` / `1.27997` / `1.08185` / `0.643873` at `tau`
240 … 10, all fourteen interior to the search, and the Student `t` beats the best zero-mean normal at
42 of 42 fits across the six populations. `ADMIT[tau]`, the fit set's 30th percentile of
`sigma_hat`, is `2.12324` … `2.18062` USD/s. No label entered any of the three tables.

**Within the domain, the shape transfers from weekday to weekday and cannot be read on the

### 2.4 What is not in the data

**Nothing in the repository is a capture**: there is no Chainlink tick stream, no Polymarket quote,
no order-book depth, no fill and no fee-paid figure in git history, and there never will be.
**Settlement readings are, where an audit route needs them:** TZ-17's report prints the venue's
published price to beat at every boundary of its 200 windows, all below the reserved boundary.

On the host, oracle data and order-book snapshots both exist. **The order book has been aggregated
once, by TZ-14, and scored twice** — by TZ-15 over TZ-14's 1,200 intervals and by TZ-16a over 2,400
later ones; every other measurement
compares the Architect's models against either withdrawn labels (§2.2) or the venue's own resolutions,
or — TZ-17 and TZ-18 — the venue's documents against one another. **Fifteen-minute books have been stored by the
chain book since 2026-09-23, and TZ-24 read them up to `1791241200`** — the containment's executable prices, against
the venue's settled documents (§2.5); the later ones are unread. **Since 2026-10-10 19:30:06 UTC the pair book
streams every change of the four books of each nested pair, beside the same seven checkpoints**, and **TZ-30 read the
streams and stored books of B3's 768 members, `1791706500` … `1792403100`**, and the `window.json` of its 936
candidates to `1792548000`; the Architect read the streams of `1792404000` … `1792722600` for TZ-32's rule, every
five-minute leg of them one of M0's or W0's markets; **TZ-32 read the streams of W1's 288 members, `1792754100` …
`1793013300`**, and the `window.json` of its 350 candidates to `1793068200`; and the Architect's audit of it read the
streams of the 289 windows it considered. **No TZ and no Architect has read a stream or a stored book of a window after
`1793013300`** (§2.5).

### 2.5 The fifteen-minute market, the chain book and the pair book

**TZ-17's documents.** The 801 settled market documents TZ-17 stored on 2026-09-22 — 200
`btc-updown-15m-{T}` for the 200 consecutive windows `T` from `1788998400` to `1789177500`, and the 601
`btc-updown-5m-*` slots from `1788998400` to `1789178400` beneath them, each named by SHA-256 in TZ-17's
committed CSV — are held by hash in `/root/btc-forensics/`, copied by TZ-18a §9 before
`/root/tz17-work/` was removed, 801 of 801 asserted. **They were byte-stable at the venue on 2026-09-23** — the
Architect re-fetched all 801 and they hashed equal at 801 of 801 — **and are no longer:** on 2026-10-09 the
Architect re-fetched the 400 documents of TZ-17's 200 windows, `btc-updown-15m-{T}` and `btc-updown-5m-{T+600}`, by
the per-slug route, and **0 of 400 hash equal to TZ-17's**, the fifteen-minute bodies now `4,914` to `5,009` bytes
against TZ-17's `5,008` to `5,103`, while **every `priceToBeat`, `finalPrice` and outcome equals TZ-17's committed CSV
at 200 of 200 windows** (§7 item 100). The largest epoch is `491,400` s below §2.3's boundary.

**What they establish** (§4). The fifteen-minute market opening at `T` and the five-minute market
opening at `T` publish the same price-to-beat literal; both families name one resolution source; each
family's outcome agrees with one reading at every shared boundary; and the reading a market settles on,
`finalPrice`, is the next market's `priceToBeat` as the same literal, for both families. **Where the
reading at `T+600` is at or above the reading at `T`, every state that resolves `btc-updown-5m-{T+600}`
Up resolves `btc-updown-15m-{T}` Up**, and symmetrically below — exactly, with no margin to carry.

**The chain book.** Root `/var/lib/btc-chainbook/` on `/dev/vda2`, outside the repository. For each
900-second window `T`: the documents of `btc-updown-15m-{T}` and `btc-updown-5m-{T+600}` at **`T+625`**,
20 s after the recorder's own fetch of that five-minute document at `T0+5`; then four `/book` reads —
both tokens of both markets, four threads released together — at seven checkpoints from `T+655` to
`T+885`, five seconds before Tier C's reads of the same five-minute market; each reply stored verbatim
with its status, byte count, SHA-256 and times, and a `window.json` at `T+905`. Thirty requests per
900 s. **TZ-18's deployment** ran from 2026-09-22 22:53:36 to 2026-09-23 06:44:00 UTC as pid `2608993`
and was refused on every request for want of a `User-Agent` (§7 item 77); its 32 windows, `1790117100`
to `1790145000`, hold two 403 documents each and no book. Its `stop` record, `wall_ns`
`1790145840007723380`, was verified by TZ-18a §0.4 together with `/proc`.
**TZ-18a's service ran** — pid `2699889`, started 2026-09-23 17:39:50 UTC, from
`/root/tz18a-svc/tz18a-chainbook-capture.py` at `ca4bbc1`, SHA-256 `e9cb9b47…`, under the exact argv
`/root/tz01-env/venv/bin/python -B -u /root/tz18a-svc/tz18a-chainbook-capture.py --serve`, appending to
`service.log`. Its proof, as of `1790187453`: all 90 requests of its first three windows served, the
84 book reads `200` and parsing as JSON, and **the four books of a checkpoint received within `1.19` to
`28.43` ms of one another, median `10.14`, over 21 checkpoints.** It stores `28,467` bytes a window,

`1791017775066372007`, counters `requests` `27,756`, `bytes_written` `24,457,260`, `asserts` `0` — 11 s after

**Reserved from its first byte, on Tier C's terms** (TZ-18 §9). No TZ but one that scores it extracts a value

### 2.6 The reward book — L0's capture, running since 2026-10-28 11:30:16 UTC (TZ-33, accepted); its streams archived daily since 2026-10-29 (TZ-34, accepted)

**One unit, `btc-rewardbook.service`,** running `/root/btc-rewardbook-svc/tz33-reward-book-capture.py` — `a276bbb9…`,
its `commit.txt` `2dcd70e…` — under `/root/tz04a-env`, root `/var/lib/btc-rewardbook/` on `/dev/vda2`. **Each UTC day
`D`** it selects 150 reward markets by TZ-33 §1's rule, from `D0 − 900` and again 60 s after a failed attempt: the
venue's reward listing ranked by daily rate, each candidate's gamma document — an end at least 15 days after `D0`, no
game, no event starting or ending in `[D0 − 1 day, D0 + 2 days)` — and its CLOB document — open, accepting orders, two
tokens, no delay, and none of 31 tags of sports and esports, crypto assets, stocks, commodities and currencies, and the
weather. **Each 900 s slot `S`** it streams the CLOB market websocket for both tokens of every member from `S − 5` to
`S + 905` — consecutive slots overlap by 10 s, so the stream has no idle stretch — and polls each member's reward
document three times, from `S + 30`, `S + 330` and `S + 630`, each round spread over 270 s; `slot.json` at `S + 910`.
**Its store**, under `<D0>/`: `<S>.<k>.stream.jsonl.gz`, one gzip member with a sync flush at least every 10 s, one
line a frame — `recv_ns`, `mono_ns` and `raw`, the pair book's line format — and one a session event;
`<S>.<k>.rewards.jsonl.gz`, one line a poll with the reply verbatim; `<S>.<k>.slot.json`; and a day's
`select.<a>.replies.jsonl.gz` and `select.<a>.json`; in the root `runtime.jsonl`, `service.pid` and `service.log`. `k`
is `1` and on for a second process in one slot. **Its bounds** (TZ-33 §1): no stream opens for a slot below
`7,300,000,000` bytes free (`STREAM_FLOOR`) or once the stream files sum to `4,000,000,000` (`STREAM_CAP`) — **summed
from their directory entries at every slot's decision, so a file removed lowers it at the next slot without a
restart**, unlike the pair book's (§7 item 106); a slot's stream is cut at `100,000,000` stored bytes; and it halts,
exit `1`, below `2,200,000,000` free at its start, a slot's start or a selection, or past `1,000,000,000` bytes one
process writes outside its streams (`WRITE_CAP`), about 61 days of one process at TZ-33's `16.3` MB a day — **a halt is
not restarted** (`RestartPreventExitStatus=1 2 3`).

**Its record:** pid `3119051` from 11:30:16 UTC on 2026-10-28; B-KILL-SVC's `SIGTERM` at 12:56:22 and pid `3137952`
from 12:56:29, `NRestarts` `1`. Its first day's selection, attempt `0`: the listing's 29 pages, 14,188 rows, 14,171
distinct, 14,103 ranked; 582 candidates walked, 218 requests, 48.1 s; 150 members — beside them 387 ending within 14
days of the day's end, 11 with an event end near the day, 7 with an end unreadable and 27 on a tag. On its first four
qualifying slots, 11:45 to 12:30 UTC, G-STREAM 4 of 4, the largest gap `1.410` s, and G-REWARDS 1,800 of 1,800 polls
served, every one with its market's row; G-NEIGHBORS HOLD; G-LOAD `0.0262` of the core (§4). **Its rate, measured over
those four slots, a Wednesday midday:** `96.0` frames a second and `10,584,064` stored stream bytes a slot,
`1,016,070,120` a day, about `121` stored bytes a frame; the venue-to-host lag of `price_change` frames a median of `15`
to `24` ms and a 99th percentile of `59` to `96` ms. Its unit's `MemoryPeak` `50,339,840` bytes over its first process,
the start-up selection included, and `35,823,616` over its second to TZ-33's close, under `MemoryMax=192M`. **The
evening selection** — the day's, from 23:45 UTC the evening before, while the stream runs — first fell due on
2026-10-28 at 23:45 UTC [its members not read by any TZ]. **To TZ-34's close** pid `3137952` ran on, `NRestarts` `1`:
the 85 slots from 2026-10-28 11:30 to 2026-10-29 08:30 UTC each closed with its streams and its `slot.json` — one in two
files, at the restart of 12:56 — and the slot of 09:15, G-NEXT's, streamed; its unit's `MemoryPeak` `60,887,040` bytes
at 07:46 UTC on 2026-10-29 and unchanged at 09:31, the evening selection included [its instant not read].

**Its streams leave the host every day — `btc-rewardbook-archive.timer`, TZ-34, since 2026-10-29 08:17:07 UTC.** At
01:30:20 UTC and 1,800 s after a start of the timer, the oneshot `btc-rewardbook-archive.service` takes every stream file
of every slot whose `slot.json` was due at least 60 s before the run's start, at most 400, uploads them to one Release a run on
the lightweight tag `rewardbook-streams-<A>` at the commit its code is from, one asset `<D0>-<S>.<k>.stream.jsonl.gz` a
file, proves each by the Release's own digest and a full anonymous download, hashes each again and removes it — every
request and every removal inside the pair book's idle stretch, every removal while the reward book is quiet, its day's
selection recorded at least 5 s before and no process of it younger than 10 s — and restarts nothing: the store's sum is read live
at every slot (above). Its state is `/var/lib/btc-rewardbook-archive/`, its ledger and each run's records under
`runs/<A>/` (§3, §6). **Its first run**, `A` `1793263627`, fired by the timer at 08:47:07 UTC: the 86 stream files of
the 85 slots `1793187000` … `1793262600`, `890,975,956` bytes, to Release `427629503`; it deferred once in its verify,
the repository then serving no Release anonymously (§7 item 128), and B-RETRY resumed it to `complete` at 09:04:30, its
86 removals at seconds 268 to 270 of window `1793264400`, `quiet_s` `0`; the reward book streamed the next slot on the
same process. **Its rate over that set**, Wednesday 11:30 to Thursday 08:45 UTC: `1,006,278,726` bytes a day. At TZ-34's
close the store held `38,055,420` bytes of streams and `/dev/vda2` `11,126,394,880` free. **Were its archive to halt**,
free space would reach `STREAM_FLOOR` within about three days, sooner before a pair book's run on its busiest days, and
the streams `STREAM_CAP` within about four [modelled, at that rate and §6's fastest base drain]; the timer's daily runs
hold the store near one day's streams, about `1.0` GB, far below both. The next run is the calendar's, 2026-10-30
01:30:20 UTC.

**Reserved from its first byte** (TZ-33 §1) — **on the host and in every Release asset made from it:** until L0's
measurement, no TZ extracts a price, size, side, book hash, rate, competitiveness or outcome from a body stored under
`/var/lib/btc-rewardbook/` or from such an asset, and no report prints one. A deployment proof reads of a stored reply
its status, byte count, SHA-256 and times, whether its body parses, and of a reward reply its shape and its row's
`condition_id`; of a stream frame its JSON shape, its event types, its token ids and the venue's `timestamp`; of a
selection its counts, its verdicts and its condition and token ids — `select.<a>.json` also carries each member's rate,
minimum size and maximum spread, which no mode prints. `slot.json`, `runtime.jsonl` and `service.log` carry counts,
times and token ids and are excepted. **The Architect's runs of TZ-33's instrument on 2026-10-27 and its snapshot of
that day precede the store and are excluded from L0's sets by time** (§5). Nothing of it enters git history. **Every TZ
after TZ-33 reads `du -sb /var/lib/btc-rewardbook` beside `df`.**

---

## 3. Code

| file | role |
|---|---|
| `research/twap-divergence.py` | the collector. Downloads and verifies the monthly archives, walks the 1-second bars, writes one row per checkpoint. The only implementation of the superseded `p_interval` arithmetic. |
| `research/selftest-twap-divergence.py` | its analytic self-tests |
| `research/tz02-distribution.py` | aggregation over the observation set. Reads the Parquet columns only. |
| `research/pfair.py` | **the pricer — the only implementation of the CANON §1.2 model, in any language.** Also the quantities it is fed: the merged stream, the time-weighted step-function mean, the one-second grid, the realised-sigma estimator, `settlement_mean`. Since `26bbb61` it carries `SD_SCALE` and `corrected_sd` — **the TZ-07b scale: one measured literal per tau, `10` included, and an unmeasured tau raises.** `G_RATIO` is removed rather than kept alongside; `c44af68` holds it in history. Imports its reader from `analyze.py` and carries no reader of its own. **Since `2746518` it also carries the Student link:** `log_t_cdf` and `t_cdf`, computed through a log-space regularized incomplete beta, `student_sd`, `p_fair_student`, and the three TZ-11a tables `ADMIT`, `LINK_NU` and `LINK_SCALE`, one literal per `tau`, `10` included; an unmeasured `tau` raises. `log_t_cdf` agrees with an independent 60-digit reference to `3e-13` relative for `\|z\|` from `0.01` to `200` at fourteen `ν` in `[2.05, 60]`, checked by the Architect; below `\|z\|` of about `1e-7 * sqrt(ν)` it returns exactly `0.5`, an error under `1e-7` in probability. |
| `research/selftest-pfair.py` | its analytic self-tests — **116 asserts since PR #12**: 56 model checks, 18 machinery checks, the 30 TZ-07b checks, TZ-10b's 6 `log_phi` checks and TZ-11a's 6 Student-link checks, every fixed expectation at `K = 100000.25` and `sigma = 3.25`. The four TZ-07a checks that encoded the superseded composition were deleted with it, none of them failing at the time. 116 of 116 reproduced by the Architect on a second machine |
| `research/tz06-calibration.py` | the Phase 1 pipeline: set formation, P, V1–V10, the gate tables. Carries no formula; calls `pfair.py` for every one. Since `1b8e4a7` `scoring_set` and `build` take `after` and `need`; at their defaults the run is the one TZ-06 ran, asserted by TZ-08a V1 to every digit of all six `Brier` values. |
| `research/tz07a-variance-time.py` | the variance–time instrument: the fifteen-lag curve, M6's disconnect rule, the in-sample diagnostics, the `gamma.json` key read. Its `G_RATIO` cross-check is removed; its `--emit-g` path still prints a table the pricer no longer has a place for. Since `2746518` `log_likelihood` and `lambda_hat` take `log_cdf=None`; at the default the committed `Φ` path runs unchanged, asserted by TZ-11a V4 to ten decimals. |
| `research/tz07b-settlement-dispersion.py` | the settlement-dispersion instrument: `Y(a, tau)` at every admissible anchor, the `Λ` table and its diagnostics, and the per-tau assert that the literal in `pfair.py` equals its own measurement to six significant digits. Imports M6's rule from `tz07a-variance-time.py` rather than reimplementing it, and opens no settled document. |
| `research/tz08a-out-of-sample.py` | the out-of-sample driver: the set assertions, the corrected and uncorrected gate tables, the scale statistic, the observations file. **Carries no threshold of its own** — G1 and G2 come from `tz06-calibration.py`, the scale statistic from `tz07a-variance-time.py`, the dispersion from `tz07b-settlement-dispersion.py`, and G3 is judged by the Architect. |
| `research/tz09-disk-inventory.py` | the disk inventory instrument: the `df` and `du` reads, the rate inventory, the unlinked-open enumeration, capture and log accounting, and R1's hash enumeration. **It cannot delete** — no deletion API occurs in it, and every `subprocess` call is a fixed argv of `df`, `du`, `git` or `journalctl --disk-usage`, with no shell. |
| `research/tz10b-sigma-or-link.py` | the sigma-or-link instrument: the intersection, `sigma_post`, `κ` through `shape`, the decile strata and the out-of-sample regression — and the helpers later instruments import: the host reads `free_bytes`, `recorder_pids`, `newest_start`, `read_set`, the grid readers `grid_of` and `sigma_hat_of`, and the label reader `m2_labels`. |
| `research/tz11a-student-link.py` | the Student-link instrument: the three sets, the member walk `walk_member`, the profile-likelihood fit of `ν` and `s` by nested golden section `fit_student`, M2's domain, the per-set refits, M4's scoring through `tz06.table_for` and `tz07a.lambda_hat(pairs, log_cdf)`, M5 and V2 … V12. **Carries no threshold of its own**; the Student likelihood and its search live here and in no other file. |
| `research/tz12-sized-gate.py` | **the sized-gate instrument, and the gate itself.** The member bootstrap of `log ν̂` and the two frozen literals `SIGMA_LOG_NU` and `SIGMA_LOG_NU_CENSORED`; the null and alternative label draws from the pricer's own `p_t`; the λ grid and `grid_lr`; `k2_of`, `crit_of`, `g4_power`, `g4_constants`, `constants`, `readings` and `verdict` — the four gates TZ-13 applies, with their error rates. **Carries no threshold of its own beyond what §4 of that TZ fixes**, reads no label, and keeps the loaded `tz11a` module so a later TZ can call §4 from it. |
| `research/tz13-sized-gate-test3.py` | **the sized gate applied once, to test 3.** Forms the set through `tz06.scoring_set`, walks it through `tz11a.walk_member`, sizes every gate through `tz12.constants` from the pricer's own predictions and writes the constants to disk, projects power at 2× and 3× the admissible rows, then reads outcomes once through `tz10b.m2_labels` and applies `tz12.readings` and `tz12.verdict` unchanged; §4's two further G4 clauses and §4.3's band are applied after, and can only weaken. **Carries no threshold of its own.** Its one departure from a committed call is the projection's synthetic `T0` keys (§7 item 61), in a table barred from every gate. |
| `research/tz14-quote-inventory.py` | **the quote inventory, and the quote machinery every later TZ imports.** `the_set` re-forms TZ-14's set with every assertion; `quote_lines` reads one quote file as the recorder wrote it, `raw` included, located by `manifest._stream_path`; `book_of` parses a reply into `Decimal` levels; `best_of` and `touch_of` give the quoted and the executable touch without depending on order; **`fee_pp` is the only implementation of CANON §1.1's taker fee**; `documents` maps token to outcome from `gamma.json`. Computes no probability. **Importing it has three side effects a later TZ names:** it writes the three thread-count environment variables; it loads `tz12-sized-gate.py`, so a later TZ takes `tz12` and everything below it from `tz14` rather than loading any of them again; and it **installs an audit hook that records the basename of every open under the capture root for the life of the process and cannot be removed** — it only appends to `tz14.CAPTURE_OPENS`. `tz14.v1_gates` asserts revision `2026-09-18-b` and twenty `frozen` rows, and no later TZ calls it. |
| `research/tz15-phase2-gate.py` | **The Phase 2 gate, on `main` since `8d2a4c6`.** Re-forms TZ-14's set through `tz14.the_set`, takes `p_t` from `tz11a.checkpoint_row` and each touch from `tz14.touch_of` at the reply's own minimum size and tick, and `select`s one share per admitted checkpoint — the larger after-fee edge, strictly positive, a tie to `Up`, the fee through `tz14.fee_pp`. **`pb_upper` is the exact Poisson-binomial upper tail**, a `Decimal` recursion at 60 digits, and `crit` its smallest critical count; both agree with an independent enumeration in rational arithmetic, and `select` with an independent re-implementation, at 0 disagreements, checked by the Architect. The labels are read once, through `tz10b.m2_labels`, behind a push check and a ledger. **Carries no threshold of its own beyond TZ-15 §4**, and its `reading` is §4's table, which §7 item 69 retires: a later TZ imports the tail, the selection and the guards, never `reading`. |
| `research/tz16a-phase2-decisive-gate.py` | **Phase 2's decisive gate, first look, and the look arithmetic the second look imports.** Loads `tz15-phase2-gate.py` once and takes `tz14`, `tz12`, `tz11a`, `tz10b`, `tz07b`, `tz06`, `pfair` and `config` from it; forms the set through `tz06.scoring_set` and the trades through `tz15.select` and `tz14.fee_pp`. **`residual_dependence` and `dependence`** give `f` and `r` from TZ-15's labelled residuals — `dependence(path)` takes the observations file's path, while `locate` searches only `/root/tz15-work/`; **`up` and `low`** read a tail at the deviation shrunk by `r`; **`critical_upper`, `critical_lower` and `look_constants`** give a look's `c`, `d` and exact error rates on `tz15.pb_upper`'s law; **`read_look`** is TZ-16a §4.1's table. `fisher_upper`, `interlock_reading` and `interlock_power` are pure. The labels are read once, through `tz10b.m2_labels`, behind a push check and a ledger, and everything computed after the read is total. **Carries no threshold of its own beyond TZ-16a §4.** On `main` from PR #20's merge. |
| `research/tz17-settlement-chain.py` | **the settlement-chain instrument, and the only committed request outside the recorder that the venue's edge serves.** Stage F fetches the venue's own market documents from the public Gamma path, paced at `0.5` s with a retry ladder, stores each body verbatim with its SHA-256 and stops at the 200th qualifying candidate; Stage A computes I1–I3 and D1–D7 from the stored bodies alone. Standard library only; imports nothing from the repository. Its request carries `Accept: application/json` and `User-Agent: btc-5m-twap-tz17` (§6). Its retry, fail-fast and early-stop paths ran only in its self-tests. |
| `research/tz18a-chainbook-capture.py` | **the chain book: the instrument and the service's code.** `build_request` sends `Accept: application/json` and `User-Agent: btc-5m-twap-tz18a`, and `http_get` stamps a receive time only where a status came back; `same_service` identifies a process by equality with its exact argv; `--prestart` proves the request path on the host and states G-TIERC's base rate before the service starts; `--serve` is the service and refuses any other command line; `--prove` reads G-DEPLOY and G-TIERC as of a fixed instant, byte-identical across runs. Standard library only; imports nothing from the repository; 39 self-test asserts, three of them against a loopback listener. Derived from PR #18's `8caa0b4` by the sixteen changes of TZ-18a §3.3 and the four helpers its report names. On `main` from PR #19's merge. |
| `research/tz20-capture-under-systemd.py` | **the capture-under-systemd instrument.** `state`, `path-recorder`, `path-chainbook`, `mark`, `unit`, `start`, `cb`, `rec`, `restart-cb`, `restart-rec`, `final` and `reclaim`: the host before the units, one live request per endpoint through each process's own functions, G-UNIT's `systemctl show` and `/proc` checks, the gates on each runtime file, and §9's copy of every file a report names by hash into the forensic store, by exclusive create. Standard library only but for `path-recorder`; an audit hook refuses any open under either capture root but the files TZ-20 §2 names. On `main` from PR #21's merge. |
| `research/tz21-recorder-memory.py` | **the recorder-memory instrument.** `state`, `synth`, `equiv`, `path`, `mark`, `restart`, `rec`, `final`, `revert` and `reclaim`: both units, both recorder trees and every copy of the three recorder files before the move; G-SYNTH, the old and the new recorder on synthetic histories, slice by slice; G-EQUIV, both on a snapshot of the capture's own `runtime.jsonl`; G-PATH, one live request per endpoint through the new recorder's own functions before the restart; the restart and G-RESTART's reading of its gap; G-REC over the first five intervals the new code closed; G-FINAL and G-CB-STILL; K-21's revert, unused; and §9's copy into the forensic store of what a report names by hash. An audit hook refuses any open under either capture root but the files TZ-21 §2 names, and any open there for writing. On `main` from PR #22's merge. |
| `research/tz23-maker-m0.py` | **M0's instrument, and the arithmetic its second look imports.** `qualify` and `candidates` form the set — the first 4,032 qualifying markets walked from `1788530400` past three excluded spans, no document requested after the need-th member; `trades` reads Data API v2 with `condition` and `taker_only` re-sent beside every cursor, every row asserted on its market and its token; `maker_terms` gives the makers' side of a taker fill in Up shares, `rebate` the 20% rebate at the reported price, `aggregate` a member's sums by band, `gate_constants` the thresholds by Ville's inequality and `reading` TZ-23 §4.3's table; `still`, `scratch` and `reclaim` as TZ-21's. Standard library only, run under `/root/tz04a-env`; every venue number a `Decimal` of its own text and every sum in the main thread; 59 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #23's merge. |
| `research/tz24-nesting-b2.py` | **B2's instrument, and the nested pair's arithmetic B3's measurement imports.** `fetch` forms the set — every window the chain book stored from `1790184600` to `1791241200`, its `window.json` and its two settled documents through gamma `/markets`, each identity re-measured on them; `constants` puts the book-free constants on disk; `score` reads the members' books once a run, behind a push check and the book ledger. `pairs_of` gives a window's dominated pair by the sign of `D`, `payoff` what the pair pays at settlement, `costs` a leg's cost after `tz14.fee_pp` collected on top of the price or in shares, `classify` the firm, loose, none and unquoted classes, `reading_of` TZ-24 §4.3's table, `theta1` and `power` NONE's alternative; `still`, `scratch` and `reclaim` as TZ-23's. Run under `/root/tz01-env`, which `tz14` needs; every venue number a `Decimal`, single-threaded; 122 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #24's merge. |
| `research/tz26-nesting-b3-capture.py` | **The pair book: the instrument and the service's code.** `serve` is the service and refuses any other command line; `run_window` runs one window — the documents through `http_get` from `T+545`, `stream_window` from `T+595` to `T+905`, `checkpoints` at the chain book's seven instants, `window.json` at `T+910`; `stream_bytes_total` sums the store's stream files once at a start, and `stream_off_reason` keeps a window's stream off for its documents, the floor or the cap; `build_request` sends `Accept: application/json` and `User-Agent: btc-5m-twap-tz26`, and the websocket handshake the same agent with no extension offered; `prestart`, `prove`, `load`, `unit`, `restart`, `final` and `restored` read the deployment; `still`, `mark`, `chainbook`, `scratch` and `reclaim` as TZ-24's. Standard library and `websockets` 17.1, run under `/root/tz04a-env`; an audit hook refuses every open, listing and process TZ-26 §2 does not give a mode; 74 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #25's merge. |
| `research/tz27-pairbook-archive.py` | **The archive's instrument, and the functions the archive's timer runs.** `inventory` takes the set as of an instant `A` — every stream file, by the service's own predicate, of every window whose `window.json` was due at least 60 s before `A`, at most 400, each hashed with its size and time unchanged; `upload_set` sends each asset whole, hashed as it is sent, and keeps one already matching; `verify_release` reads the Release's own record — `state`, `size`, `digest`, URL — and downloads every asset, and `release_reading` is G-UPLOAD and G-DOWNLOAD; `delete_set` hashes each file again and removes it, logged at once, and `removed_reading` reads it back; `restart_reading`, `counter_reading`, `unit_reads` and `resume_decision` are G-RESTART, G-COUNTER, G-UNIT and G-RESUME; `stream_bytes_view` mirrors the service's sum; `credential` reads the token from the primary checkout's remote URL and prints only its source; `GitHub` and `build_request` fix every header, `User-Agent: btc-5m-twap-tz27`. An audit hook, installed at import, enforces TZ-27 §2 by its `MODE` and `WORK`. Standard library alone, run under `/root/tz04a-env`; 63 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #26's merge. |
| `research/tz28-pairbook-archive-timer.py` | **The archive on a timer: the code its service runs, and the deployment's instrument.** `run`, the service's one mode, refuses any start but systemd's and any directory but the archive's state; it loads `tz27-pairbook-archive.py` from beside itself, executed from the bytes whose SHA-256 it asserts, and calls TZ-27's `inventory`, `upload_set`, `verify_release`, `delete_set`, `restart_reading`, `counter_reading`, `unit_reads` and `resume_decision` under TZ-27's own audit hook, whose mode it sets for each step; `idle_wait` holds every request to seconds 15 to `590 − budget` of a window, its budget `5 + ⌈bytes / 2,000,000⌉` s, and `Gh28` times each into `runs/<A>/transfers.jsonl`; `Ledger` appends one record a step to `ledger.jsonl`, `pending` resumes a run without a terminal record, a failure before the removal defers — exit `75` — and the third deferral of one set, or any failure from the removal on, halts the archive and disables its timer until a TZ appends `clear`. `fingerprint`, `selftest`, `tz27-release`, `path`, `path-token`, `installed`, `mark`, `timer`, `first` and `final` read the deployment. Standard library alone, run under `/root/tz04a-env`; 34 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #27's merge. |
| `research/tz29-maker-m0-second-look.py` | **M0's second look.** `fingerprint`, `selftest`, `still`, `scratch`, `archive`, `rehearse`, `replay`, `fetch`, `constants`, `score` and `reclaim`; it loads `tz23-maker-m0.py` from beside itself, executed from the bytes whose SHA-256 it asserts, and calls TZ-23's `candidates` and `form_set`, `fetch_chunk`, `member_rows`, `build_constants`, `gate_constants`, `score` and `reading` for the walk, every sum, the thresholds and the reading. Its own code is the first look read from the forensic store, the sum over both looks, a fetch whose every chunk is held to the pair book's idle stretch and away from the archive's run — `quiet` and `hold` — and the archive's ledger read and printed, `ledger_summary`. Standard library alone, run under `/root/tz04a-env`; 51 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #28's merge. |
| `research/tz30-nesting-b3-measure.py` | **B3's measurement.** `fingerprint`, `selftest`, `still`, `scratch`, `archive`, `rehearse`, `fetch`, `strikes`, `score`, `rescore` and `reclaim`, run under `/root/tz01-env`, which `tz14` needs. It executes `tz24-nesting-b2.py` from the bytes whose SHA-256 it asserts, and through it `tz14-quote-inventory.py`, whose bytes it asserts first, and calls TZ-24's `form_set` — and through it `chainbook_window`, `window_problem`, `doc_problem` and `identities` — `gamma_cached`, `slug15`, `slug5`, `pairs_of`, `payoff`, `costs`, `classify`, `LEGS`, `read_books` and `line_check`, which parses a reply with TZ-14's `book_of`, and TZ-14's `fee_pp`, `touch_of` and `best_of`; its mode `strikes` executes `pfair.py` and the recorder's `config`, `manifest` and `analyze` the same way, never TZ-24, and calls `analyze.reports`, `analyze.first_at_or_after`, `config.interval_dir` and `pfair.price_to_beat`. It writes no second implementation of any of them. Its own code is the replay of a window's streams at venue time — every book rebuilt, every attempt of the capture rule placed, filled, unwound and settled, G-REBUILD's comparisons against the stored replies — the strikes' receive stamps, the archive's assets and Release downloads, the units held to the idle stretch, the lag, the reading over eight blocks, G-STILL's count of the archive's own restarts, and TZ-29's `quiet`, `hold`, `still`, `scratch` and `reclaim`, written again for its own hook; 42 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #29's merge. |
| `research/tz31-who-earns.py` | **W0's instrument.** `fingerprint`, `selftest`, `still`, `scratch`, `archive`, `rehearse`, `fetch`, `constants`, `score` and `reclaim`, run under `/root/tz04a-env`. It executes `tz23-maker-m0.py` from the bytes whose SHA-256 it asserts and calls TZ-23's `candidates` and `form_set` for the walk, `trades` for both feeds of a market's fills, `aggregate` — and through it `maker_terms` and `rebate` — for every position, cost and rebate, `gate_constants`, `reading` and `label_of`; it writes no second implementation of any of them. Its own code is the makers' rows of a market — the full feed less the takers', as multisets, `split_feeds` — each wallet's own position by TZ-23's `maker_terms` on its side flipped, `own_rows`, the two named groups' sums over each market's life and the pool beside them, the takers' fee, G-STILL with the archive's own restarts read from its ledger, and TZ-29's `quiet`, `hold`, held fetch, `scratch`, `archive` and `reclaim`, written again for its own feeds; 58 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #30's merge. |
| `research/tz32-our-quoting.py` | **W1's instrument.** `fingerprint`, `selftest`, `still`, `scratch`, `archive`, `rehearse`, `fetch`, `replay`, `rescore`, `score` and `reclaim`, run under `/root/tz04a-env`. It executes `tz23-maker-m0.py` from the bytes whose SHA-256 it asserts and calls TZ-23's `gamma_docs` and `http_json` for every document, `qualify` for every leg, `aggregate` — and through it `maker_terms` and `rebate` — for every position, cost and rebate, `gate_constants`, `reading` and `label_of`; it writes no second implementation of any of them. Its own code is the replay of a window's streams at venue time — `Window`: the five-minute leg's Up book rebuilt from the stored frames, a match read from the Up token's own book frame of the same stamp, `sweep`'s consumption from the best level outward, our quotes queued behind the displayed size or first at `front`, filled by a match or, once the 150 ms taker delay has passed, by a crossing order, refused where they would cross on arrival, cancelled through a one-way latency, the quoting rule in `decide` — under nine `VARIANTS`, two gated; the stream and leg rules over `window.json` and the documents; the downloads from the archive's Releases held to the pair book's idle stretch; `still_reading`'s chain of the archive's restarts; 74 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #31's merge. |
| `research/tz33-reward-book-capture.py` | **The reward book: the instrument and the service's code.** `serve` is the service and refuses any other command line, an untuned glibc or a second instance; `run_rule` evaluates TZ-33 §1's selection through a fetcher — `LiveFetch`, every request through `http_get` and every try stored, or `StoredFetch`, each request answered by the reply an attempt stored — so the stored replies alone re-derive a day's set; `stream_slot` streams a slot and `StreamWriter` stores each frame and session event, and the stream floor and the live cap keep a slot's stream off; `poll_rows` and `poll_served` read a reward poll; `build_request` sends `Accept: application/json` and `User-Agent: btc-5m-twap-tz33`, and the websocket handshake the same agent with no extension offered; `prestart`, `prove`, `resume`, `load`, `unit`, `restart`, `final` and `stopped` read the deployment; `fingerprint`, `selftest`, `still`, `mark`, `scratch` and `reclaim` as TZ-32's. Standard library and `websockets` 17.1, run under `/root/tz04a-env`; glibc's mmap threshold fixed at 128 KiB and its arenas at two at import; an audit hook refuses every open, listing and process TZ-33 §2 does not give a mode; 99 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #32's merge. |
| `research/tz34-rewardbook-archive-timer.py` | **The reward book's archive on a timer: the code its service runs, and the deployment's instrument.** `run`, the service's one mode, refuses any start but systemd's, any directory but the archive's state and `-O`; it loads `tz28-pairbook-archive-timer.py`, and through it `tz27-pairbook-archive.py`, from beside itself, executed from the bytes whose SHA-256 it asserts, rebinds TZ-27's audit hook to the reward book's store and installs its own beside it; `inventory` takes every stream file of every closed slot whose `slot.json` was due at least 60 s before the run's start, at most 400, each hashed; TZ-27's `upload_set` and TZ-28's `Gh28`, `verify_phase`, `Ledger`, `pending`, `halt` and idle stretch do the upload, the verify and the record; `remove_set` hashes each file again and removes it inside the idle stretch while `quiet` reads the reward book quiet, and `hold` waits while the pair book's archive runs. A failure before the removal defers — exit `75` — and an audit refusal, the third deferral of one set, or any failure from the removal on but the quiet's timeout halts the archive and disables its own timer until a TZ appends `clear`. `fingerprint`, `selftest`, `path`, `path-token`, `installed`, `mark`, `timer`, `first`, `final`, `stopped` and `still` read the deployment, with TZ-27's `scratch` and `reclaim`. Standard library alone, run under `/root/tz04a-env`; 49 self-test checks. On `main` from PR #34's merge, with this revision. |
| `deploy/systemd/btc-recorder.service`, `deploy/systemd/btc-chainbook.service` | **the two system units**, installed byte for byte under `/etc/systemd/system/`: `Restart=always`, `RestartSec=5`, `StartLimitIntervalSec=3600`, `StartLimitBurst=5`, each process's deliberate exits excluded — `RestartPreventExitStatus=0` for the recorder, `1 2 3` for the chain book — `MemoryMax=512M` and `192M`, output appended to `recorder.log` and `service.log`, `WantedBy=multi-user.target`. On `main` from PR #21's merge. |

| `research/recorder/recorder.py` | the live capture, Tier A and Tier C in one process. Read-only: no order path, no CLOB authentication, no credential, no pricing arithmetic. **Since `0f6c9045…`, PR #22's merge, it keeps two hours of runtime records** — the clock samples of the last two hours, every `start` record and the disconnects that end inside them — where `9fd1c7de…` kept every record it had read or written for the life of the process (§6, memory). A slice older than that is read from disk, and every slice is the old code's, byte for byte (§4). |
| `research/recorder/config.py` | every constant the capture uses, each traced to the TZ that fixed it |
| `research/recorder/manifest.py` | the per-interval manifest, a pure function of the interval directory |
| `research/recorder/analyze.py` | the TZ-04b analyzer and the only stream reader — `reports`, `last_at_or_before`, `first_at_or_after`, `venue`, `published_equal`, `scoring_set`. Repaired by TZ-06 R-c; runs. |
| `research/recorder/probe.py` | the Tier B order-book probe, run once |
| `research/recorder/selftest.py` | the recorder's self-tests — 115 asserts, each aborting the run |
